IPv4 compares numerically via `ntohl`, IPv6 via `memcmp` over the 16 network-order bytes; both reduce to an unsigned bytewise compare, which is what I implemented.\n- SHA-1 over the packed buffer, then `SCBase64Encode` in strict mode, which is standard base64 *with* padding (`/app/rust/src/ffi/base64.rs:178`), prefixed `1:`.
